    37 Stop adding rules to the equivalent-hemisphere reduction of fem, in which every audit round found new limit cases (an interface 1 cm below a deep ring +191 … +308 % without a message; a rod reaching 1 µm … 8 cm into a more conductive top layer −44 … −78 %, warning only)? yes; no further rules, replaced by a conforming axisymmetric model (rod on the axis, ring, ring with central rod; other electrodes refused until a full 3-D method exists); planned for a later release on 2026-11-08, moved into 2026.11.0 with the full 3-D model by the user decision of 2026-11-14 implemented in 2026.11 (streams w4axi, w4fem3d; fixes of audits W4A / W4B in fw4axi, fw4fem3d): conforming axisymmetric model of rods on one axis and rings around it (about 1e-4; rings next to a conductive layer 3.3e-4 since W4A-01), and a three-dimensional conforming model for every other world — strips, grids, meshes, several clusters, galvanic distributed conductors — within 2e-3 of the axisymmetric model away from interfaces and other wires (W4B-01/-02) and between image_2layer and mom at the AP1 station; the 3-D port limit is the option FemOptions.max_ports_3d (stream fw4int); the equivalent-hemisphere reduction is gone (changelog)
    38 Which owner model stays when concrete_model is removed: one current per rectangle without cross-braces (up to 2.2 % above the reference with one current per segment) or one per side (up to 0.9 %; short side at least 1 m)? one per side, already in 2026.11.0: concrete_model has no effect stream d3gen
    39 Record the effective-resistivity preset in the BusType document (for example metadata["rho_preset"]), so that a bus paired with the wrong preset is detectable? yes, in a later release (2026-11-08); moved into 2026.11.0 by the user decision of 2026-11-14 done in 2026.11.0 (streams w3perf, fw3perf, fw3r2b): rho_preset of the exporters, metadata["rho_preset"], BusTypeSpec.rho_preset, the last line of the description of the live BusType and of its database entry (bustype_rho_preset; audit W3B-06), RhoPresetMismatchWarning from evaluate_spec / bustype_from_spec; a document holds a known preset name or null, and only an exact preset line of a description is replaced (audit W3R2B-06); older documents load with None (changelog)
    40 Quantify the overlap of the concrete shells at the corners and crossings of encased foundations? only once a 3-D volume reference is available quantified in 2026.11 (stream w4fem3d, audit W4B-05) against the concrete resolved as a volume in the 3-D fem model: a junction acts like a few cm of wire, at most about 2 r_b, sign not settled (not converged); the generators' house mesh changes by about 1 % or less (0.35 … 0.85 % re-measured after W4B-01); no correction rule (concrete shells)

    The audits of stream d3gen (D3A-02, D3A-03, D3B-03, D3B-04) found that the generators attach PEN conductors at the connection point of a foundation's anchor, which is a corner for rectangular foundations in this release (meshes since audit A2G-04, rings since Q38; the centre of one grid mesh in 0.15.0) and the first vertex for building outlines; the author decided on 2026-11-14:

    Q Question Decision Where
    41 Where do the conductors the generators route to a foundation end: at the centre as in 0.15.0, at the corner of the anchor side, or at the point of the foundation nearest to the conductor? at the nearest point, for every foundation form (rectangles, building outlines, the substation's foundation), already in 2026.11.0 streams f4gen, f5gen, f6gen (audits D3A, D3B, R4A, R4B, R5A, R5B, R6A); rings and strips since Q43 and layout cables clipped at every site they pass since audits R6A-01, W1B-03, W1B-04 (streams w1gen, fw1gen) (changelog)

    The hand-over of 2026-11-14 left three questions; the author answered them the same evening and decided to integrate (almost) every open roadmap item into 2026.11.0 before the release — everything scheduled for the next release, the items left open, and the full 3-D FEM; out of scope stay the BranchType export to groundinsight, the import of measurement setups from groundmeas, the AP1 reference-case library and scaling the LU solve to 10 000 segments.

    Q Question Decision Where
    42 Route service drops around foundations (street geometry) instead of the shortest connection? no, keep the shortest connection no code change; known limitations, roadmap (row of the TN generator)
    43 Apply the nearest-point rule of Q41 also to sites earthed by a RingElectrodeSpec circle or a strip (the default substations)? yes done in 2026.11.0 (stream w1gen): every conductor routed to such a site ends at the point of the electrode nearest to it (a circle exactly), rods keep their head (changelog)
    44 One current per straight wire for building outlines? yes, together with Q30 (one current per wire line in the solver) done in 2026.11.0 (stream w1sol); straight runs at projected coordinates since audits W1A-01 and W1R2-05 (streams fw1sol, fw1r2sol), in linear time since audit W1R3-03 (stream fw1r3) (changelog)

    The decisions D1–D9 taken while these items were implemented are listed in Decisions of 2026-11-15, before the sections of the four waves at the end of this page.

    Correction to question 38 (audit D3B-01)

    The 0.9 % of question 38 was measured at the cases where the owner change is largest. Over the whole scope of the owner texts (short side at least 1 m, aspect ratio up to 50, sides up to 150 m, the AP1 soils) one owner per side is at most 1.4 % above one current per segment (150 m × 3 m bare in 30 over 200 Ω·m: +1.34 %) and one owner per rectangle up to 2.2 % (16 m × 1 m in a 0.2 m shell of 30 Ω·m over 1000 / 30 Ω·m: +2.106 / +2.160 % at 0.5 / 0.25 m segments); one per side is closer everywhere, so the decision stands.

    Corrections (adversarial audits of the decision streams)

    Two adversarial audits of the three decision streams below (D2A / D2B, fixed by the streams f2solver and f2gen; R2A / R2B, fixed by f3solver and f3gen) corrected these statements; the release notes describe the release:

    • d2solver, Q23: the rule also covers a more conductive layer below the hemisphere but within the electrodes' horizontal half-extent when no more resistive layer lies in between (the error is continuous across a_eq, −67.9 % on both sides for a 10 m ring over 1000/30 Ω·m; an extension by the coordinator, for the author's review). A horizontal wire touches the layers that hold its axis (within its wire radius only for the rule of an electrode below its hemisphere); a rod end on an interface does not touch the layer beyond. fem misplaces the current by −97 … +30 % in the rejected worlds (mostly far too low); more resistive spanned layers are −3 … +48 % off, not +3 … +45 %; none of the rejected worlds was silent before, they warned. fem also refuses strips deeper than half their length, rings deeper than their diameter, driven MeshElectrodes and electrodes and galvanic conductors out of the soil, and names only backends whose own checks accept the world.
    • d2gen, Q20: the brace table is measured against the bare braces of the development line, which were never released; in 0.15.0 the braces had the shell radius without the shell resistance, and against the 0.15.0 "distributed" model the brace rule alone is +5.9 / +66 / +393 % at ρ_c = 150 / 1000 / 5000 Ω·m. The hand assembly that reproduces the solve is a check of the generator with the same kernel and owner model, not an independent method (an independent boundary- element model confirms the numbers; with one current per segment the brace effect is +49.2 / +318.5 % instead of +46.9 / +307 %).
    • d2gen, Q21: concrete_model changes the impedance of an axis-aligned rectangle without cross-braces (also style="mesh" with n_x = n_y = 1) by less than 1 % up to aspect ratio 4 and 2 % up to 50 over the AP1 soils (short side at least 1 m; 5 mm wire at 0.8 m, bare or in concrete of 30 … 1000 Ω·m up to 0.2 m thick; largest values found 0.69 / 1.55 %), not by at most 0.16 % / 0.67 %, and the premise of question 21 (≤ 0.06 %) holds only for bare rings in homogeneous soil (0.09 % / 0.33 % measured): flagged for the author's review.
    • d2soil, Q36: the medians 1.8 % / 5.0 % are over all 39 AP1 soils with the homogeneous ones at zero (2.1 % / 5.5 % over the 36 two-layer soils); that the hand-over's 1.7 % / 15.7 % are this metric is an inference (groundfield 0.14.0 itself at 1.5 m segments reproduces them).

    Stream d2solver: User decisions Q23, Q27, Q31: fem and more conductive layers, perfect_mirror across the surface, per-engine size notice¶

    Changed¶

    • ⚠ fem (experimental) raises BackendCapabilityError when the equivalent hemisphere of a cluster spans a layer that no electrode of the cluster touches and that conducts better than every layer the electrodes touch (user decision Q23, finding 10.23). The hemisphere injects the current straight into that layer and the result is too low: −97 … +5 % against image_2layer and mom in the measured cases (rods, strips and rings below a more conductive top layer from 22 mm thickness, rings above a more conductive lower layer; −92 % for a 10 m ring at 0.8 m over 1000/30 Ω·m at h₁ = 2 m). Up to now a FemReductionWarning or silent. The rule is structural (near-equal 100/120 Ω·m raises as well), uses the canonical layer stack, and an electrode exactly on an interface touches both layers; the message names the cluster, the layer, the electrodes' ρ, a_eq, the measured range and a backend that solves the world. More resistive spanned layers keep the warning (+3 … +45 %). Tests: tests/test_fem.py::test_fem_rejects_a_more_conductive_layer_its_hemisphere_spans and four siblings.
    • earth_inductive_model="perfect_mirror" emits one UserWarning per solve when inductive conductor segments lie on both sides of the soil surface, naming the number of such pairs and the conductors and pointing to "sommerfeld" (user decision Q27, finding 10.46): its additive image has no physical basis for an overhead/buried pair (2.28 / 2.77 times the mutual reactance of sommerfeld at 50 Hz / 1 kHz for a lead 1 m above a PEN 0.7 m deep). Numbers do not change. Tests: tests/test_inductive_solver_integration.py::test_perfect_mirror_warns_for_inductive_pairs_across_the_surface and siblings.

    Added¶

    • Engine.warn_segments (int | None, default None = diagnostics.SOFT_LIMIT, 5 000, read at solve time): the size of the soft notice "solve time may run from seconds to minutes" per engine, counted like max_segments (segments and nodal unknowns); honoured by the solve's pre-flight warning, check_segment_resolution and diagnose() (report.context["warn_segments"]). Not checked against max_segments (a notice above the hard limit never appears). Booleans rejected; kept by JSON, copies and the provenance (user decision Q31, finding 10.06). Tests: tests/test_resource_limit.py::test_warn_segments_is_a_validated_engine_field and four siblings.

    Numbers that change¶

    What Size Who is affected Golden cases
    none (Q23 turns results into errors, Q27 adds a warning, Q31 moves a notice) — fem users with a conductive layer spanned by the hemisphere get an error instead of a −97 … +5 % result none

    Stream d2gen: User decisions Q20, Q21: encased mesh braces, concrete_model deprecated¶

    Changed¶

    • ⚠ Mesh foundations: the cross-braces are encased (user decision Q20). A foundation earth electrode per DIN 18014 lies in the concrete together with its connections, so every wire of an encased FoundationElectrodeSpec(style="mesh") — the perimeter and the cross-braces — carries the outer radius \(r_b = r_a + t\) and the shell coefficient \(C\); up to now the braces stayed bare, and a foundation could not exceed the spreading resistance of its braces alone however dry the concrete. Without a shell nothing changes. See Numbers that change. Tests: tests/test_concrete_one_mechanism.py::test_mesh_braces_are_encased, ::test_encased_mesh_equals_the_assembly_of_its_wires, ::test_shell_of_a_mesh_foundation_spans_every_wire.
    • groundfield.generators.materialise.concrete_shell takes encased_length_m (the keyword perimeter_m of the earlier 2026.11 work is gone) and reports the shell resistance \(C/L\) over every encased wire.

    Deprecated¶

    • FoundationElectrodeSpec.concrete_model (user decision Q21). The field no longer selects a shell model; it only chooses one current owner ("lumped", the default) or one per side ("distributed") for an axis-aligned style="ring" rectangle, at most 0.16 % apart up to aspect ratio 4 and 0.67 % up to 50 over the soils of the AP1 grid, bare or in concrete of 30 to 1 000 Ω·m (0.06 % / 0.3 % for a bare ring in homogeneous soil). "distributed" keeps working and emits a DeprecationWarning at the user's line (constructor, model_validate, JSON, model_copy that sets it); "lumped" and the configuration JSON and pickles of 0.15.0 load silently; the generators do not repeat the warning. Drop the argument; removal no earlier than 2027.5. Tests: tests/test_foundation_owners.py.

    Numbers that change¶

    | Q20 (10.13) | the cross-braces of an encased mesh foundation carry the concrete shell like its perimeter (DIN 18014; user decision Q20) | default house mesh (10 m, 2 × 2, 0.8 m, r_a 5 mm, t 5 cm) in 100 Ω·m −1.1 / −0.03 / +1.5 / +47 / +307 % at ρ_c = 50 / 100 / 150 / 1 000 / 5 000 Ω·m, in 30 / 100 Ω·m (h₁ = 5 m) +1.3 / +6.2 / +12 / +154 / +861 %; medium_industry mesh (20 m, 3 × 3) −1.3 … +298 % and +1.0 … +671 %; no golden case moves | generated mesh foundations with concrete_rho_ohm_m set; not worlds without a shell (the default building catalogue), not ring and building-outline foundations, not AP1 |

    Stream d2soil: User decision Q36: effective-resistivity presets per electrode class¶

    Added¶

    • Effective-resistivity presets per electrode class (user decision Q36). gf.effective_resistivity(soil, preset=...) takes the weights \((w_0, \beta)\) from the read-only mapping groundfield.soil.EFFECTIVE_RESISTIVITY_PRESETS, with the values of the AP2 hand-over (2026-10-20, AP1 catalogue computed with groundfield 0.14.0): "lv_network" (0.13, 0.8; the default, the AP1 calibration), "station" (0.06, 1.3; the station electrode alone) and "lv_group_<g>_p<pp>" for the four LV network groups (perimeter equivalent \(n_U\), thresholds 20 / 100 / 250) and 5 / 10 / 20 / 40 % foundation electrodes (method A, q50). An explicit w0 or beta replaces that weight only; an unknown name raises ValueError naming the closest presets. For the AP1 station in the 39 AP1 soils, \(R = c\,\rho_\text{eff}\) misses the field solution by a median 1.8 % and at most 15.7 % with "station" (hand-over: 1.7 %, 15.7 %), against 5.0 % and 35.9 % with "lv_network". Tests: tests/test_effective_resistivity.py (presets section, test_the_station_preset_reproduces_the_handover_for_the_ap1_station, test_the_documented_tables_hold_the_presets).

    Changed¶

    • effective_resistivity: w0 and beta default to None, the value of the preset; every call keeps its value bit for bit (default preset "lv_network" = 0.13, 0.8). The IO reference example fits the station over \(\rho_\text{eff}\) with preset="station" (max_relative 7.48 % instead of 13.41 %); Soil reference, ADR-0008 (amendment item 5) and glossary describe the presets and that a BusType and its bus must use the same one.

    Numbers that change¶

    none (no library value moves; golden unchanged).

    Streams f2solver, f2gen, f3solver, f3gen: fixes from the audits of the decision streams¶

    Two adversarial audits attacked the work of the three decision streams; four fix streams answered them. Their notes were written as instructions for the release notes, which now carry the result (the box above lists what they corrected); in short:

    • f2solver (audit D2A): one contact predicate for the layer rules of fem; Q23 within the electrodes' horizontal half-extent; documented ranges re-measured; a remedy only from backends whose own checks accept the world; electrodes out of the soil refused like in every backend; strips and rings deeper than their formulas and driven MeshElectrodes refused; conductor names with a line break in the perfect_mirror warning; which entry points emit the size notice.
    • f2gen (audit D2B): encased mesh foundations measured against 0.15.0 (the bare braces existed only on the development line); the concrete_model bound with its scope and rectangles without cross-braces; model_copy validates concrete_model; the shell helper over every encased wire; the hand-over's station metric marked as inferred.
    • f3solver (audit R2A): only a rod is non-horizontal (round-off in a strip's end depths); the wire-radius band only for the rule it makes stricter, Q23 and the A2E-2 rule on the wire's axis again; no Q23 error for a conductive layer behind a more resistive one within the half-extent; ring depth limit at the diameter; galvanic conductors out of the soil refused; remedies with the setting that lifts a refusal; ranges with converged image series.
    • f3gen (audit R2B): the concrete_model bound with the short side in both clauses, a margin over the largest values found and its model uncertainty; model_copy of subclasses; copy.replace warns at the caller's line on Python ≥ 3.13.

    Stream d3gen: User decision Q38: one current owner per side¶

    Every rectangular foundation without cross-braces (style="ring", or "mesh" with n_x = n_y = 1) is one strip per side, bonded to its local −y side, at every orientation; FoundationElectrodeSpec.concrete_model keeps its validation and loads from JSON but has no effect ("distributed" warns, "lumped" stays silent). Up to this change the default "lumped" made an axis-aligned rectangle one GridMeshElectrode. The release notes carry the details; in short:

    • Size: the input impedance is never higher and unchanged for a square on its own. Over the AP1 soils (short side at least 1 m) the largest decreases are −0.32 / −0.91 % bare or with the default shell and −0.69 / −1.53 % in up to 0.2 m of wet concrete (aspect ratio up to 4 / 50), confirmed by the side-matrix algebra and an independent boundary-element solver; the AP1 station −0.0009 … −0.0026 %.
    • Connection point and names: conductors attached to the foundation by name start at the corner of its −y side instead of its centre, and the electrode named after the foundation is that side (cluster_impedance for the foundation), as for meshes and rotated rectangles.
    • fem: the four strips are reduced as a cluster; no new rule (user decision Q37), the changes are listed in the fem row of the release notes and in the known limitations.
    • Golden: only mutual_foundations_lumped_concrete moves (Z_G 5.2e-4, R 1.6 %, structure of the world), now equal to the distributed case.

    Stream f4gen: User decision Q41 and the fixes of audits D3A, D3B¶

    Every conductor that TnNetworkGenerator and the layout builder route to a foundation ends at the point of that foundation nearest to the conductor: a service drop is the closest pair of its feeding cable and the building's foundation wires, a cable leaving a site with a foundation starts at the foundation's point nearest to its next node, and the conductor is attached to the wire that holds the point. Ties go to the lowest wire index, then to the smallest arc position. Sites without a foundation keep their connection point, and so does everything attached by name. The first version joined a cable that crosses a foundation to it without a drop; the audits of the stream completed that rule (see the f5gen and f6gen sections). In short:

    • Drops: of the 282 drops of audit D3A's ring-house set (11 968.5 m), 47 ran along their own foundation and 45 across it; the first version had 264 drops (10 529.6 m), none along or across its own foundation, but 18 radial substation-slot drops on the trunk; now 246 drops (9 963.5 m), none on a cable (closest pair checked against 2 mm sampling).
    • Networks (seed 1, 100 Ω·m, default grid; values after the audit fixes, see the f5gen section): the resistive PEN at 50 Hz moves by −0.005 … −0.12 % (star) and −0.03 … +0.13 % (radial); with a Neumann PEN at 1 kHz |Z| by −0.71 … +3.3 % (star) and −0.67 … +9.0 % (radial). The 12 m × 8 m ring houses of audit D3A have +5.7 … +14.0 % more reactance than drops to the centre as in 0.15.0; the default 10 m ring networks with drops +1.2 … +2.1 % (star, 30 houses) and −10.8 … −1.1 % (radial).
    • A layout of building outlines with an AP1-like station: −0.026 … −0.147 %, drops 215.9 → 140.6 m. The AP1 catalogue itself attaches its drops and bonds by name and is not changed by Q41 (audit R4B-14).
    • Fixes: right angles are exact rotations and keep the anchor side (D3B-04, D3A-05); non-positive foundation sides raise (D3A-01); diagnose() leaves out the pairs of one foundation's wires that meet at its corners (D3A-04; since audit R4A-04 only wires bonded by the foundation's _bond_ conductors and only pairs at a shared vertex); the per-side bound is 1.4 % (D3B-01, see the correction above); the texts on the connection point, the golden field change and the fem ranges are corrected (D3A-03, D3A-06, D3B-02, D3B-03, D3B-05 … D3B-07).
    • Golden: gen_tn_default (≤ 2.9e-4), gen_layout_builder (≤ 1.1e-3) and gen_tn_measurement_overhead_leads (≤ 1.6e-4, the probe reading 4.3e-3) move; the other 46 cases are bit-identical.

    Stream f5gen: Fixes of audits R4A, R4B (user decision Q41)¶

    The audits of stream f4gen found three routing paths that broke the rule — drops that started on the foundation of a house the cable crosses (R4A-01), radial drops from the substation that lay on the trunk through the houses it crosses (R4A-02, R4B-01: 6 drops over 195 m in the default 30-house network), layout taps inside the station foundation (R4A-03) — an over-broad diagnose() grouping (R4A-04), unchecked plan contacts (R4A-05), a slower planner (R4A-06) and scoped or stale numbers (R4B-02 … R4B-14). In short:

    • Crossings: a building that a cable crosses is joined to it and fed by it, without a drop: a cable of its feeding path, every trunk of the radial topology, and in the star topology the main that crosses it, whose cabinet then feeds it (coordinator decision: no cross-ties between cabinets through a house; 1 of 50 and 3 of 150 default houses change cabinet). No drop runs along a cable farther than one minimum section (1 cm): a drop from a node that would run along a trunk or another main starts where it leaves it (needed by the random stress set). The guard, with the router's 1 cm tolerance since audit R5A-01, finds no drop along a cable in the default grids and audit D3A's set; in the 400 random networks one drop shares the 1 cm of rule 3's bound (a foundation 8 mm beyond a cabinet rod at the cable's end), which the guard allows.
    • Numbers, scoped (seed 1, 100 Ω·m, default grid; sommerfeld and carson_series, two segment lengths): Q41 moves resistive networks by −0.005 … −0.12 % (star) and −0.03 … +0.13 % (radial) at 50 Hz; with a Neumann PEN at 1 kHz Im −3.1 … +16.3 % (star; the 10-house network with 5 of its houses on the main +14.2 / +16.3 %) and −8.6 … +37.7 % (radial; default 30 houses +10.4 %). Radial networks react to where the drops end much more than star ones.
    • Other fixes: diagnose() groups only the wires bonded by a foundation's _bond_ conductors and leaves out only pairs that meet at a shared corner; plan contacts off their wire, unknown or on a rod are rejected; planning one cabinet feeding 1000 houses 3.14 → 0.89 s; LvNetworkLayout.plot(world=...) draws the drops as built; one owner up to 2.2 %, the scanned 145 m × 2.9 m rectangle, the passive-field change +5.5 / −4.7 % and the provenance of the touch-voltage values corrected.
    • Golden: nothing moves against f4gen.

    Stream f6gen: Fixes of audits R5A, R5B (user decision Q41)¶

    The second audit round found one regression of the first round's fixes and three smaller routing gaps — layout taps next to a rotated, polygon or offset station or cabinet foundation were computed on the slanted first section and refused, so the drops fell back to the station, the cabinet or the far end of the cable, 14.0 … 36.5 m instead of 6.0 m (R5A-02); "along a cable" was tested to 1 µm, so a drop 2 µm beside a trunk ran 60 m along it (R5A-01); a cable touching a house at its end node and crossing it elsewhere gave it a 1 cm drop (R5A-03); radial planning grew faster than the network (R5A-04) — and texts that stated the pre-fix rules or unscoped numbers (R5B-01 … R5B-13). In short:

    • Layout: the taps stay on the layout polyline, clipped at the projection of the site contact onto it; no tap on the first or last segment inside a site foundation (a cable corner inside one stays a node, audit R6A-01); audit R5A's 40 random layouts: drops more than 1 cm longer than the closest pair of the built cable 30 → 6 (e91f3c0: 7).
    • Routing: "along" means both ends of a cable section within one minimum section (1 cm) of the drop's line, the guards use the same value; a crossing beyond a touching node is the joint; set lookup (radial 8000 houses with max_feeder_length_m=1e5 43.6 → 18.4 s, plans bit-identical). The default grids, audit D3A's set and the 260 random networks of the stress tests are unchanged.
    • Drops over other foundations: 0 … 80 % of the drops of the default grids run 0.2 m above other houses' side wires; moved 1 m aside they change |Z| by at most 0.02 % (insulated PEN) and −0.30 % (bare PEN). Routing drops around foundations was left to the next release; the author then kept the shortest connection (user decision Q42).
    • Golden: nothing moves.

    Verification audit R6A of stream f6gen¶

    A focused third round on the fixes of stream f6gen reproduced them (the layout clip of R5A-02 bit for bit against e91f3c0 in the auditor's five cases, the 1 cm rule changing only the 73 near-collinear drops of the stress set, the joint search, the bit-identical set lookup, and the sensitivity of drops over other foundations: bare PEN −0.30 %, insulated +0.02 %, two discretisations each). It found that a router corner inside a small cabinet ring stays a node that houses tap (R6A-01: 82 of 2 540 drops in 200 random layouts, also before user decision Q41) — documented as a known limitation and scheduled in the roadmap — and five scoping and wording points (R6A-02 … R6A-06: an evidence line of the stream's report, the max_feeder_length_m=1e5 of the 8000-house timings, the definition of "along" in the 10.38 row, the frequency of the detour reactance, the substation drop in tutorial T06), which the release notes, the known limitations and the tutorial now state.

    Decisions of 2026-11-15¶

    While the items of the user decision of 2026-11-14 were implemented, eight questions came up (D1–D8), each with a recommendation. On 2026-11-15 the author followed every recommendation and asked, for D6, to stay with licences that allow further commercial use and are as free as possible. During the waves he decided two more: the form of the far images of the two-layer image series and D9. The table keeps the question, the recommendation that is now the decision, and where it was done.

    id Question Decision Status and where
    D1 Give VoltageSource an internal impedance Z_q = R + jωL (two fields, one diagonal entry of the source's constraint row)? Without it: a junction with a conductor, or the 1 A solve scaled by hand. yes (small, useful for AP2) done in 2026.11.0 (stream fw2src): internal_resistance_ohm, internal_inductance_h, 0 by default and then bit-identical to the ideal source; pickles written before it load as ideal sources, and a galvanic mom world factorises once for all frequencies (audits W2R2-01, W2R2-02, stream fw2r2) (changelog)
    D2 Make the near/far switch of the line kernel continuous (a deterministic margin or a far form exact to round-off), so that results do not depend on where a world stands? It moves worlds near the origin, estimated at about 2e-7 (audit W2B-02: 1.15e-5 for the switch alone in golden line_img2_rod_crosslayer). yes, in stream w3xl (the numbers move far below every tolerance; reproducibility wins) done in 2026.11.0 (stream w3xl, item 5): a C¹ blend between 6 and 7.5 segment lengths; 1e-13 over local shifts, ≤ 5e-11 at UTM coordinates (audit W3A-04); with the far images and the cross-layer line form of the same stream the golden line-kernel cases move by up to 6.0e-4 (changelog)
    D3 A current that varies along each wire in the image family (stars +1.8 … +10.4 %, 8 arms in 1000/100 Ω·m +12.3 %; grids +0.36 … +0.70 % above one current per segment)? roadmap (mom is the per-segment answer today) open: roadmap
    D4 The generator rules wave 1 left open: a group rule for wide groups (about 180°), one cabinet around the substation, a cabinet that cannot keep its clearance (it raises; it could move on along its ray)? roadmap; the raise is a clean failure open: three items of the roadmap
    D5 Worlds that mix projected (UTM) and local coordinates are not helped by the shifted local frame of stream w2mom: what to do about them? document them, once audit W2B has quantified them quantified by audit W2B-04 and done in 2026.11.0 (stream fw2mom); by the coordinator's decision on W2B-04 beyond the text: diagnose() reports such a world under coordinates, and its solve warns with MixedCoordinatesWarning (known limitations, changelog)
    D6 Dependencies of the 3-D FEM as an optional extra of the MIT package (meshers: gmsh GPL-2.0-or-later with exceptions, netgen LGPL, TetGen AGPL; scikit-fem BSD-3, pyamg MIT)? permissive licences only (MIT / BSD / Apache): scikit-fem with its own tensor-product meshes and pyamg; no GPL / AGPL; a weak-copyleft mesher only if nothing permissive works, and then ask again done in 2026.11.0 (stream w4fem3d): the optional extra fem3d with scikit-fem (BSD-3-Clause) and pyamg (MIT); the design needs no surface-fitted mesh, so no external mesher and no copyleft package (changelog)
    D7 A continuous cabinet rule instead of the 20 m threshold, which moves a cabinet by up to 142 m for a 0.2 m change of the house spacing (X at 1 kHz about −20 % in the 50-house grid; AP1 mixes with groups at 18.8 / 20.2 / 20.7 m; audit W1R2-02)? keep the threshold for 2026.11, document the step and record the rule per cabinet; the AP1 catalogue does not use the generator's cabinets done in 2026.11.0 (stream fw1r2gen): the plan records PlannedSite.cabinet_rule and PlannedSite.centroid_ray_gap_m per cabinet, the known limitations give the step (141.8 m, X at 1 kHz −21.6 / −18.9 % with carson_series / sommerfeld); a continuous rule is on the roadmap
    D8 Rebase the golden baseline for 2026.11.0 after the last wave, since the declared bounds of many streams add up and no longer bind (audit W1R2-07)? yes, as the last step before the release dry run done in 2026.11.0: the baseline regenerated from the code of 2026.11.0 (563 quantities in 48 cases); the declarations of the cycle moved to tests/golden/expected_changes/released/2026.11.0/
    D9 Service drops that leave one tap in one direction (the radial taps, the substation drops of the star) lie on one axis, where the inductance matrix reads every pair as touching (diagnose(): 10 overlapping pairs in the 5-house radial network, 247 with 200 houses): keep them, or lay them side by side? in a row side by side at the touching distance; only neighbours touch done in 2026.11.0 (stream fw3perf; along one common normal under placement jitter since audit W3R2B-01, stream fw3r2b): PlannedBranch.axis_offset_m; X at 1 kHz of the 5-house radial network −2.33 / −2.70 % (carson_series / sommerfeld); no generated network reports an overlap for jitter_m 0 … 0.1 m; no golden case moves (changelog)
    far images Which form do the far images of the two-layer image series take (stream w3xl, item 1)? the quadrupole form stays the default (the reference model); an Engine option gives the point form for fast parameter studies done in 2026.11.0 (stream fw3xl): Engine.image_far_field = "quadrupole" \| "point"; the TN 40-house network in 1000/30 Ω·m with h₁ = 1.5 m solves in 0.49 of the default's time; since audit W3R2A-01 (stream fw3r2a) the point form keeps the near image pairs on the cross-layer path, and a 75 m strip below the interface is within 1.1e-5 of its exact value at any segment length (up to −58 % before) (changelog, known limitations)

    Waves 1 to 4 of the extended release¶

    On 2026-11-14 the author moved (almost) every open roadmap item into 2026.11.0 (see the decisions above). The work ran in four waves, each with implementation streams on disjoint files, the coordinator's texts, a first round of two adversarial audits, fix streams with the coordinator's decision per finding, and a second audit round that attacked the fixes, again followed by fixes; wave 1 had a third, verifying round, and the rounds of wave 4 after audit W4A follow at the release. The audits number their findings per round (W1A-01 … W4A-11). Wave 1 with its first fixes and audit W1R2, and the wave-2 streams, ran in the cloud workspace (Linux x86-64, OpenBLAS); the rest ran on the author's Mac (macOS arm64, Accelerate), where every claim of bit identity is made against a golden baseline generated on the same machine. As for the streams above, the release notes carry the result; the sections below say what each stream and each audit round did, with the numbers as corrected by the later rounds.

    Wave 1: one current per straight wire, concentric cables, cabinets per building group¶

    Wave 1 implemented the user decisions Q30 / Q44 and Q32 / Q33 in the solver and the coupling (stream w1sol), and Q22 / Q35, Q43 and the layout fix of audit R6A-01 in the generators (stream w1gen); Q42 needed no code. Its audits ran three rounds: W1A / W1B, W1R2 on the fixes, and the verification round W1R3.

    Stream w1sol: one current per straight wire, concentric cables by marker (Q30, Q44, Q32, Q33)¶

    image, image_2layer (with its cross-layer path) and solve_mutual_* give every grid one uniform-current owner per full wire line, a star one per arm and a polyline one per straight run; the owners of an electrode are ideally bonded, every result stays per electrode, and metadata["wire_owners"] counts them. Rods, strips and rings stay one wire; generated mesh foundations, one electrode per wire line already, do not change. In the coupling, a thinner conductor on the axis of a thicker one is a core inside a shield only when the thicker one has conductor_type="cable_shield", and a shield has no internal inductance of a solid wire. In short:

    • Grids: −1.2 % (10 m, 2 × 2 meshes) … −4.0 % (40 m, 8 × 8) … −5.5 % (100 m, 20 × 20) in 100 Ω·m; above one current per segment +0.36 … +0.70 % instead of +1.6 … +4.9 %. Against mom, at the corner of the 20 m grid with 4 × 4 meshes: touch voltage +6.4 % (+20 % before), step voltage −7.6 % (−21 %); the touch-voltage envelope of the 10.21 note 111.44 → 98.72 V.
    • Outlines and stars: a building outline is one owner per side and equals the rectangle given by its size (rectangle outlines −0.000 … −0.31 % bare, −0.62 % in wet concrete; the T06 layout with the AP1 station −0.03 … −0.40 %). A star alone does not change (equal arm currents); its gap to one current per segment stays on the roadmap (decision D3). A source-free grid, star or polyline floats at one potential with exactly zero net current.
    • Concentric cables: two unmarked conductors on one axis are separate cables that touch (a 100 m pair of 5 mm and 15 mm radius: loop 31.97 → 43.47 µH, the analytic 43.48 µH); the self terms of a shield drop μ₀ℓ/(8π) (50 nH/m), and the 100 m coax reads the analytic 26.97 µH instead of 31.97 µH. buried_lead() is a shield: two-pole readings with buried leads −2.5 % in reactance.
    • Golden: img2_grid_upper_resistive (Zc −0.94 %), cim_grid_unequal_segments (−1.41 %), img_mixed_cluster_hom and line_img_mixed_cluster_hom (−0.20 %, electrode currents up to 3.4 %) move; Q32 / Q33 move no stored case. The refinement bound of benchmark B11 is 0.6 % instead of 0.5 %.

    Stream w1gen: cabinets per building group, the nearest point on rings and strips (Q22, Q35, Q43, R6A-01)¶

    The star topology of TnNetworkGenerator orders the buildings by bearing around the substation, from the widest angular gap on, cuts them into one run of consecutive bearings per cabinet (sizes differ by at most one) and places each cabinet on the ray through its group's centroid, 1 m (CabinetConfig.end_clearance_m) beyond the group. In the radial topology a building in a substation slot taps its feeder's trunk (Q35). Every conductor routed to a site earthed by a ring or a strip, the default substations among them, ends at the point of that electrode nearest to it (Q43), and a layout cable reaches a site where its polyline enters the site foundation (R6A-01). In short:

    • Default networks: the 30-house star had both cabinets west of the substation and fed its eastern houses by drops of 3.5 … 106.5 m from the substation (25 drops, 1 140 m); its cabinets now stand at (−118.5, 0) and (118.5, 0) m with 20 drops of 25 m. The 30-house radial network: drops 1 115 → 778 m, 14 → 4 of them from the substation. The default mains leave the substation ring at its point nearest to their cabinets.
    • Numbers (default grids of 10 … 150 houses, 100 Ω·m, with the fixes of fw1gen): resistive PEN at 50 Hz star +0.12 … +0.85 %, radial −0.01 … +0.49 % (Q43 alone −0.05 … −0.13 %); an isolated Neumann PEN at 1 kHz (up to 50 houses) star |Z| −1.4 … +13.1 %, radial −1.2 … +15.4 %. The reactance of the default 30-house star rises by +64 / +55 % (carson_series / sommerfeld), because its houses share their main instead of parallel drops (audit W1B: the rise comes from the tree, +52 % in an own lumped model, +54.9 % with mom).
    • Golden: gen_tn_default (substation potential +1.18 %, the current of the cabinet rod −62.7 %: the rod stands 1 m beyond the houses instead of 62.5 m) and gen_tn_measurement_overhead_leads (+1.76 %, the probe rod's potential +117 %) move; the other 47 cases are bit-identical.

    Audits W1A and W1B of the wave-1 streams¶

    Two auditors attacked the merged wave 1 (snapshot 6f63deb, change from e6124cd) with code of their own, W1A the solver and coupling side, W1B the generators. Findings:

    • W1A-01 (medium): the absolute collinearity tolerance (a sine of 1e-9) split the wire lines of rotated generated meshes at UTM coordinates into extra owners (a 10 m mesh with 10 × 10 meshes: 142 extra owners, −0.55 %; up to −0.64 %).
    • W1A-02 (medium): the coax loop inductance shrank with the number of pieces (26.966 µH lumped, 24.608 µH in 0.25 m pieces, −8.8 %).
    • W1A-03 (low): the 10.21 row still gave 69.6 V at +x; since Q30 it is 59.95 V (point kernel), 61.31 V (line kernel), 61.58 V (mom).
    • W1A-04 (low): the owner texts of the generators described one leakage current per electrode.
    • W1A-05 (low): model_copy bypassed the rule that a shield has no skin model.
    • W1A-06 (low): diagnose() counted one owner per electrode (102 instead of 393 unknowns).
    • W1A-07 … W1A-10 (info): a lone floating split electrode reported -0-0j; the roadmap had lost the star item and three decision rows of this page were stale; two documentation points on shields.
    • W1B-01 (medium): a group spanning about 180° got its cabinet in empty ground (10 houses and one small commercial building: 50.4 m from the nearest building, drops 527 instead of 337 m).
    • W1B-02 (medium): the clearance looked only at the group's horizontal wires; a cabinet rod stood at the point of a building's rod.
    • W1B-03, W1B-04 (low): the R6A-01 clip covered only a cable's own end sites, and its merge cut off cable legs that serve houses (drops 30.0 → 142.8 m).
    • W1B-05 … W1B-08 (low): end_clearance_m without a lower bound, golden bounds wider than their quantities and adding up across streams, stale texts, tests that did not check what they promised.

    Confirmed: one owner per wire line equals bonded strips to 7e-15 in every engine path; an independent grid code gives the owner gaps (20 m 4 × 4 grid: +3.297 % with one owner, +0.574 % with one per wire; stars +1.81 / +3.48 / +10.54 %); 54 records outside the items are bit-identical; 26.97 µH and 43.48 µH follow from closed forms; over about 300 layouts every PEN network is one tree, every building is fed once and every drop is the closest pair; ring contacts lie on the circle within 2.5e-13 m. Taken up by fw1sol (W1A except -04 and -08), fw1gen (W1B and W1A-04) and the coordinator (W1A-08: the roadmap item and the rows).

    Streams fw1sol and fw1gen: fixes of audits W1A and W1B¶

    • fw1sol (W1A): a vertex continues a straight wire when it lies on the chord of its neighbours up to 64 units of round-off of the coordinates (COLLINEAR_ROUNDOFF_ULPS), so generated meshes at UTM coordinates read their local value again (+0.09 … +0.64 %). Pieces of conductors lying beside each other add up: a thinner wire parallel inside a shield couples at the shield radius at every axial offset, and the coax reads 26.97 µH in any parallel pieces (it read 26.91 / 26.37 / 24.61 µH in 10 / 1 / 0.25 m pieces), the unmarked pair 43.47 µH. A copied skin shield is rejected at the solve, diagnose() counts 393 unknowns, a lone floating electrode reports 0j, and the 10.21 note gives the touch voltage after Q30. Golden: cond_drops_along_pen_main_perfect_mirror moves (X at 1 kHz +3.9e-4, Zin 1.3e-5).
    • fw1gen (W1B, W1A-04): a cabinet stays on its centroid ray, end_clearance_m beyond the group's farthest electrode (rods included) plus the plan extent of its own grounding, unless it would stand there farther than max_far_end_gap_m (20 m) from the nearest electrode of its group; then it stands on the ray through the group's far-end building. This hybrid rule is the coordinator's: the stream's first version put every cabinet on the far-end ray, which gave the 50-house grid six drops from the substation. The case of W1B-01 has its cabinet 1.56 m from the nearest building and 311.8 m of drops. A cabinet that cannot keep its clearance to another site raises, naming both; end_clearance_m is positive. Layout cables are clipped at every site they pass, and only the leading and trailing waypoints inside the end sites are merged.
    • Numbers (fw1gen): resistive PEN at 50 Hz of the default star grids of 100 … 200 houses −0.031 … +0.089 %, the mix of 10 houses and one small commercial building +0.489 %; the wide mixes with an isolated Neumann PEN at 1 kHz |Z| +0.3 … +1.9 %; layouts with a cable through a passed cabinet −0.16 … −0.45 %. The 10-, 30- and 50-house grids, the radial networks and every golden case are unchanged.

    Audit W1R2: round 2 on the wave-1 fixes¶

    The second round attacked the fixes of fw1sol and fw1gen (snapshot 1579ed8). Findings:

    • W1R2-01 (medium): the rule of W1A-02 acted only on pieces parallel within 4.47e-5 rad; at 4.5e-5 rad the 100 m coax read −0.17 … −7.42 % again in 10 … 0.25 m pieces, with a step at the tolerance.
    • W1R2-02 (medium): the 20 m threshold switches a cabinet between two positions up to 142 m apart (the 50-house grid with its house columns 32.0 / 32.2 m apart: X at 1 kHz about −20 %); the texts said it separates the group populations, which it does not (decision D7).
    • W1R2-03 (low): the bundle rule evaluated all piece pairs at once (two 600 m conductors: 30 → 292 MiB).
    • W1R2-04 (low): texts against the measurements (the AP1 gaps are 20.21 … 53.56 m, not 23.7 … 50.4 m; thresholds of 15 / 25 m move two groups of the 100-house grid, not one; a timing of the first version).
    • W1R2-05 (low): straight runs were judged per vertex triple, so a near-duplicate vertex or a finely sampled arc counted as straight at UTM coordinates.
    • W1R2-06 (low): rows 22 and 35 of this page still said "next release", and five rows linked a roadmap section that no longer listed them.
    • W1R2-07 … W1R2-10 (info): the golden bounds of several streams add up (decision D8); without conductor labels unmarked touching pieces keep the old drift; end_clearance_m ≥ max_far_end_gap_m silently sent every group to the far-end ray; World.check() let a copied skin shield through.

    Confirmed: the coax and the unmarked pair add up exactly at local and UTM coordinates (1.6e-15), the W1A regressions and the ten W1B tests pass, the layout clip is clean in 17 hand-made attack cases, and the cabinet rule is deterministic and raises nowhere over the 80 AP1 mixes. Taken up by fw1r2sol (01, 03, 05, 08, 10), fw1r2gen (02 with D7, the code texts of 04, 09) and the coordinator (the other texts of 04, 06; 07 by D8).

    Streams fw1r2sol and fw1r2gen: fixes of audit W1R2¶

    • fw1r2sol: pieces of two conductors tilted against each other get the bundle rule, with the stretches inside, beside and away from the partner found exactly (the length gate and the angular cap of this version went in fw1r3); the tilted coax returns to its lumped value (+0.004 … +8.0 %). The generator's overhead probe lead, drawn from 10 mm beside the feed lead to its axis, is such a pair: the probe junction potential moves by 6.5e-5 … 1.1e-4. The bundle rule needs memory in proportion to the matrix (292 → 30 MiB, bit-identical). Straight runs grow against the chord of the whole run (a 2° arc electrode of R = 50 m at UTM coordinates +1.27 % → +5.5e-4 against the local origin). World.check() rejects a copied skin shield. Golden: nothing moves.
    • fw1r2gen (D7): the 20 m threshold stays and the step is documented with its measured size (the 50-house grid: 141.8 m, X at 1 kHz with an inductive PEN −21.6 / −18.9 %, carson_series / sommerfeld); the plan records per cabinet which rule placed it and the gap it compared (PlannedSite.cabinet_rule, PlannedSite.centroid_ray_gap_m). Over the 80 AP1 mixes 118 groups stand 20.21 … 53.56 m from their houses on the centroid ray and take the far-end ray; the largest gap kept is 18.84 m. end_clearance_m must be smaller than max_far_end_gap_m. Golden: nothing moves.

    Verification audit W1R3 of the W1R2 fixes¶

    A third round checked the fixes of fw1r2sol (snapshot 8c83d7c). Its own integration of the documented readings agreed with the code on 1740 piece pairs to 5.5e-12 wherever the code claims a reading. Findings:

    • W1R3-01 (medium): the length gate of the tilted rule kept the old drift for longer pieces: a core crossing the shield's axis stepped by −8.6e-5 of the loop at the gate, shield pieces of mixed length −1.6e-3, where the texts said about 1e-6.
    • W1R3-02 (low): the cap |cos φ| ≥ 0.99 made a new step of 1.5e-3 at 8.1° for two wires leaving a junction in 0.02 m pieces.
    • W1R3-03 (medium): the chord rule took quadratic time: 4000 collinear edges 13.3 s (0.085 s before W1R2-05), a 2 km polyline electrode in 1 m vertices 17.7 s (0.46 s).
    • W1R3-04 (low): a row of Numbers that change said "more than" 0.46 µm, where the rule changes polylines whose vertices lie closer than that.
    • W1R3-05 … W1R3-07 (info): correct but undeclared moves (wires leaving a junction at 3° in fine pieces +0.098 / +0.75 %, drops routed back along a split main +6.8e-5 … +1.65e-3); the partition into runs depends on the vertex order at UTM coordinates (2.4e-8); the carson_series gap between a lumped tilted pair and its pieces grows with the square of the tilt (4.2e-4 … 4.4e-3 in X at 2e-3 … 8e-3 rad).

    Taken up by fw1r3 (01, 02, 03; 06 measured), the coordinator's texts (04, 05) and stream w3perf of wave 3 (07).

    Stream fw1r3: fixes of audit W1R3¶

    • Tilted rule: no length gate and no angular cap; pairs within 1e-9 of orthogonal count as orthogonal. Every case of the audit adds up within 2.2e-12, the tilted coax within 6.4e-12 for tilts up to 1e-3 rad (an unmarked touching pair within 3.1e-10), and the solved loop within 1.1e-15 under perfect_mirror (sommerfeld within 1.6e-8, its own piece dependence).
    • Chord rule in linear time: the decisions are the same bit for bit, and the runs are cached on the vertices' values: 4000 collinear edges 0.006 s, the 2 km polyline electrode 0.25 s instead of 17.3 s. The order dependence of W1R3-06 stays at 2.37e-8.
    • Golden: strict against the stream's own baseline, 49 cases pass; of a probe of 178 arrays 177 are bit-identical, and two wires leaving one point at 30° in 0.05 m pieces move from −1.04e-4 to −7.8e-16 off their lumped pair. Every generated network is bit-identical.

    Wave 2: impressed voltage sources, shells in mom, projected coordinates¶

    Wave 2 added impressed voltage sources (stream w2src, finding 10.51, the open item "voltage sources" of the scope of 2026-11-14) and concrete shells in the moment method, together with solves at projected coordinates that do not depend on where a world stands (stream w2mom, an open point of fw1sol). Decisions D1 and D5 came with its fixes.

    Stream w2src: impressed voltage sources (10.51)¶

    A VoltageSource holds attached_to at U above return_to (or remote earth) and is solved by modified nodal analysis: one current unknown and one constraint row per source in the nodal system of the shared pipeline, in image, image_2layer (also on the cross-layer path), mom and mom_sommerfeld, with conductors, inductive coupling and several frequencies. Up to 0.15.0 every backend dropped it with a warning and solved to zero potentials. In short:

    • Results: SourceRecord.current holds the solved current (new SourceRecord.voltage and column voltage_v), input_impedance() is U/I, and sweep, the rho-f helpers, split_factor and touch_voltage_spectrum accept the source; several sources superpose and 0 V is an ideal short; new capability BackendCapabilities.voltage_sources.
    • Checks: voltage sources that close a loop through ideal bonds or remote earth, and a source short-circuited by an ideal bond, raise ValueError naming the sources; fem rejected voltage sources with BackendCapabilityError until its conforming models of wave 4, which solve them.
    • Validation: the voltage solve equals the 1 A current-source solve scaled by U/Z_in in every backend, in two- and three-layer soil, on the cross-layer path and with inductive conductors at 0 / 50 / 1000 Hz (at most 1.4e-15), and agrees with solve_mutual_matrix for a two-terminal source, the hand-inverted transfer matrix of three sources, Dwight's rod (−0.012 %) and the hemispherical far field.
    • Golden: no stored quantity moves; worlds without voltage sources are bit-identical.

    Stream w2mom: concrete shells in mom and mom_sommerfeld, solves at projected coordinates¶

    • Shells: each segment adds its radial shell resistance C/Δs to the diagonal of the point-matching system and the shell drop to its potential; with one owner per segment the image family solves the same system (7e-16), and a ring rises by exactly C/(2πr) in one, two and three layers (1e-14). Up to then both engines raised BackendCapabilityError; fem remained the only backend that refused shells, until wave 4. mom is thereby the per-segment reference for encased foundations: the image family's point kernel lies +0.35 … +0.69 % above it for grids in 5 cm of wet concrete and up to +18.8 % for stars.
    • Projected coordinates: a world with a horizontal coordinate beyond 10 km is solved in an exactly shifted local frame and reported in user coordinates, also by FieldResult.potential, and the segment counts allow the round-off of the coordinates' own magnitude (64 ε|x|): a generated 10 m mesh foundation at UTM coordinates had 508 instead of 440 segments, and mom / image(kernel="point") moved by 2.6e-4 / 2.8e-4, now 3e-12; the line kernel stayed within its own spread (1.5e-6; ≤ 5e-11 since decision D2). A distributed conductor whose length is a whole number of pieces up to round-off gets no extra piece (a 60 m counterpoise at UTM coordinates 7 → 6 pieces).
    • Golden: nothing moves; the AP1 catalogue is not affected. Three open points went to the audits: the knife edge of the line kernel's switch (D2), worlds that mix coordinates (D5) and the brace effect with mom.

    Audits W2A and W2B of the wave-2 streams¶

    Two auditors attacked wave 2 (snapshot ecff7b6, change from 1579ed8), W2A the voltage sources, W2B the shells and the frame. Findings:

    • W2A-01 (medium): LvNetworkLayout.measured_grounding_impedance and verify_current_balance fell back to 1 A for a voltage source (5.604 instead of 1.635 Ω, balance ok=False).
    • W2A-02 … W2A-09 (low): compare_engines left out the input impedance of voltage sources; the alias _warn_ignored_sources lost its stacklevel (TypeError in every world); a voltage record without voltage and current gave [] or IndexError; "equals the scaled 1 A solve" holds for one source only (two sources −7.5 %); the messages of a same-node voltage source and of a loop; a direct solve_fem and the shell refusal of fem named the wrong capable backends.
    • W2A-10, W2A-11 (info): source_kind="voltage" with a measurement set-up solved, but measured_impedance refused it; a bullet of this page and notebook 83.
    • W2B-01, W2B-02 (medium): the near/far switch of the line kernel is a knife edge: two-layer image pairs on it spread Z by 1.0e-5 … 2.6e-5 and the touch voltage by up to 5.5e-5 with the position of a world (the texts said up to 2.4e-7), also through the series-image switch, whose far form is the point form; the estimate of D2 (about 2e-7) was wrong (golden line_img2_rod_crosslayer 1.15e-5).
    • W2B-03 (medium): "worlds within 10 km unchanged" is false for distributed conductors: a 60 m / 10 m counterpoise goes 7 → 6 pieces in 876 of 3600 directions, Z −0.30 % (image) and −2.54 % (mom).
    • W2B-04 (medium): decision D5 quantified: the round-off grows only from 1.0e-11 to 1.5e-11 (point kernel), but nothing warns; a return electrode given at local coordinates raised Z_in by 0.43 %, a cable end given locally made a 33 118 km conductor (7.5 kΩ instead of 5.4 Ω), and sommerfeld did not finish.
    • W2B-05 (medium, pre-existing): one lumped Neumann conductor under sommerfeld took 0.42 / 4.3 / 28 s and 116 / 364 / 1647 MB at 0.25 / 1 / 2 km.
    • W2B-06, W2B-07 (low, texts): the brace effect with mom and the medium_industry values were missing from the concrete-shell page; "mom_sommerfeld equals mom to 4e-9" holds for one soil only (1.4e-9 … 1.5e-8 over four).
    • W2B-08 (info): the frame shift is exact only for coordinates between o/2 and 2o, o being the frame's origin; elsewhere it is within 1.9e-9 m.

    Confirmed: the modified nodal analysis matches the scaled 1 A solve and independent closed forms to 1.1e-15 in every backend, layering, terminal kind and frequency tested; worlds without voltage sources are bit-identical (563 of 563 golden quantities) and cost the same; the shell term and the frame agree with two independent methods each to round-off (the rise C/(2πr) of a ring to 5.6e-15; 19 worlds at 10 offsets: Z within 2.6e-11). Taken up by fw2src (W2A-01 … -10, with D1), fw2mom (the texts of W2B-01, -03, -04 with D5, -06 … -08), stream w3xl of wave 3 (W2B-01, -02 by D2), stream w3perf (W2B-05) and the coordinator (W2A-11).

    Streams fw2src and fw2mom: fixes of audits W2A and W2B, decisions D1 and D5¶

    • fw2src (D1): VoltageSource.internal_resistance_ohm and internal_inductance_h (≥ 0, 0 by default: the ideal source, bit for bit) and internal_impedance(f); Z_q is the diagonal entry of the source's constraint row in every backend that solves voltage sources, and input_impedance() is the impedance the source sees, U/I − Z_q. A galvanic world whose source has an inductance is solved per frequency on the complex path. Validated against the junction + lumped_series_resistance_ohm pattern (≤ 7.2e-16), the 1 A solve with I = U/(Z_q + Z_in) in every backend, three earth models and the cross-layer path (≤ 4.0e-15), and the transfer matrix of three sources and solve_mutual_matrix (≤ 3.4e-16). A source with R > 0 opens a loop of voltage sources; an inductance alone does not.
    • fw2src (W2A): the layout readings use the solved current (1.635 Ω); a record without voltage and current raises ValueError; stacklevel is restored; the loop message names the sources on the loop, and a same-node voltage source is reported as short-circuited; compare_engines compares the input impedance of voltage sources; source_kind="voltage" gives the readings of "current". Golden: 563 of 563 quantities identical; worlds with ideal voltage sources bit-identical.
    • fw2mom (D5, W2B): diagnose() has the category coordinates (a warning when points lie both within 10 km of the origin and beyond 1000 km from it, naming both groups and the conductors between them; information for a world wider than 100 km that does not mix), and such a solve warns once with MixedCoordinatesWarning before anything is discretised. The texts give the measured line-kernel spread (W2B-01), the local piece counts with W2B's pin test (W2B-03), the brace effect with mom (+49.6 / +321.2 % for the house mesh, +49.5 / +313.7 % for medium_industry at ρ_c = 1000 / 5000 Ω·m; the owner model with bare braces 1.6 / 2.8 % and 2.2 / 3.6 % high), the agreement of mom_sommerfeld with mom (about 1e-8), and where the frame shift is exact. Golden: 563 of 563 quantities identical.

    Audit W2R2: round 2 on the wave-2 fixes¶

    The second round attacked the fixes of fw2src and fw2mom (snapshot 34ad269). D1 holds (the constraint row, its sign and the per-frequency complex path agree with independent methods to round-off; ideal sources stay bit-identical), the W2A fixes hold, and D5 raised no false alarm in eleven projected and local coordinate systems while it caught every stray node or conductor end of a UTM world; worlds outside the items are bit-identical (563 of 563). Findings:

    • W2R2-01 (low): worlds and results pickled before D1 raised AttributeError.
    • W2R2-02 (low, cost): a galvanic mom world whose source has an inductance got one complex factorisation per frequency (×2.0 / ×8.1 / ×28 at 1 / 5 / 20 frequencies, 1488 segments).
    • W2R2-03 (low, text): the stated direct-pair spread of the line kernel was exceeded (a 12 m strip 5.9e-7 / 1.15e-6).
    • W2R2-04, W2R2-05 (low): the message of a short-circuited source with R > 0; measured_impedance raised "2 sources" for a result with one voltage and one current source, which read the current source before.
    • W2R2-06 … W2R2-11 (info): "never flagged" is untrue for wide pure worlds; a TN network with its substation at UTM coordinates builds a mixed world (D5 warns, correctly); "876 of 3600" depends on how the angles are formed (829); mom_sommerfeld against mom is 9e-8 with the interface 0.2 m below the wires and 9.2 % with a truncated series (both warned); a large R loses log10(R/Z_E) digits, and 1e-310 Ω gave nan; what the D5 rule does not see (a stray point between 10 and 1000 km, field and probe points).

    Taken up by fw2r2, the known-limitation texts through the coordinator.

    Stream fw2r2: fixes of audit W2R2¶

    • Pickles: worlds and results pickled before D1 load as ideal sources (W2R2-01).
    • Cost: a galvanic mom / mom_sommerfeld world whose source has an inductance factorises its segment system once and solves only the V × V system of the source currents per frequency (exact): 1.02 times the solve without the inductance at 20 frequencies on 1 488 segments, 28 times before; results equal to 3.3e-15 (W2R2-02; audit W3B: within 1.4e-14 of the former path, ×1.04 … ×1.05).
    • Messages and readings: a short-circuited source behind Z_q is told that its current U/Z_q would circulate through the bond and the source; measured_impedance and the layout readings take the single current source, else the single voltage source, as up to 0.15.0 (W2R2-04, -05). A non-zero R or L below the smallest normal float is rejected (W2R2-10), and SubstationConfig.position is documented as local (W2R2-07).
    • Texts: the line-kernel spread, the scope of D5, the 829 … 876 directions and the agreement of mom_sommerfeld (W2R2-03, -06, -08, -09); the text on what D5 does not see (W2R2-11) went to the known limitations.
    • Golden: 563 of 563 quantities identical.

    Wave 3: the two-layer image series, earth return and soil¶

    Wave 3 took the roadmap items of the two-layer image series and the cross-layer path with decision D2 (stream w3xl), and those of the earth return, the soil and the BusType document with the user decisions Q34 and Q39 (stream w3perf). The author decided the far-image option and D9 during its fixes.

    Stream w3xl: far images, the cross-layer path in line form, memory, the continuous switch (D2, 10.28, 10.56)¶

    Five items. In short:

    • Far images (item 1): far Tagg/Sunde images carry the quadrupole term of the segment average, and the far form of segment pairs the radius term of the reduced kernel; the term is tapered off between 60 and 120 segment lengths of the pair's vertical distance and left out below 1e-9 of a unit point term. A straight strip in two-layer soil is within 3.6e-6 of its uniform-current resistance for segment lengths of 0.125 … 2 m, not monotonically (+5.1e-4 before; the 7.7e-7 of the stream held at its five sampled lengths only, audit W3A-03), and the surface potential above it within 8e-6 (0.94 % before).
    • Cross-layer path (items 2–4): the layered correction in line form (its exact image expansion, ADR-0007 amendment): a rod through the interface changes by 9e-7 between segment lengths of 2 and 0.125 m (1.3e-4 … 2.4e-4 before), and the two paths of image_2layer agree to 1.7e-7 where both apply (6.2e-4). Memory as on the series path, in row chunks and in row blocks beyond the budget: 278 → 137 MiB at the AP1 station with 40 % penetration with numba (without numba 224 → 140 MiB since audit W3A-05). The spectral amplitudes in closed form: the path with the point kernel 1.6 … 2.0 times faster at the AP1 station, round-off.
    • Continuous switch (item 5, D2): pairs between 6 and 7.5 segment lengths blend the exact and the far value with a C¹ weight (direct pairs, surface and Tagg/Sunde images, the images of the cross-layer correction, field points): 1e-13 over local shifts and ≤ 5e-11 at UTM coordinates (4.7e-11 measured by audit W3A-04), where the position had moved results by up to 5.9e-7 (direct pairs) and 8e-6 … 2.6e-5 (two-layer images).
    • Numbers: homogeneous soil ≤ 7.8e-7; two layers: house grids 3e-6 … 1.8e-4, wires near the interface up to 6.2e-4 (audit W3A-04), surface potentials near the interface up to 0.9 %; cross-layer rods ≤ 4e-4, the AP1 station −4e-5. The cost was larger than the stream stated (audit W3A-01, see fw3xl).
    • Golden: 60 quantities in 9 line-kernel cases declared (w3xl.toml), up to 6.0e-4 (line_img2_ring_rods_within_layer); line_img2_rod_crosslayer moves 4.0e-4, its former point-form error. Point-kernel and mom-family cases are bit-identical apart from the round-off of the closed-form amplitudes (≤ 2.5e-16).

    Stream w3perf: penetration depth, sommerfeld segment rules, the BusType preset, overlaps (Q34, Q39)¶

    Roadmap items of the earth return and the soil, the user decisions Q34 and Q39, and two audit leftovers (W2B-05, W1R3-07). In short:

    • Penetration depth: penetration_depth(soil, frequencies) and PenetrationDepth give the complex penetration depth and the equivalent resistivity of a soil with any number of layers; exact for homogeneous soil, over the 39 AP1 soils (50 Hz … 1 kHz, 0.1 … 1000 m) within a median of 2.2 % of the exact layered earth return (at most 28 / 33 % overhead / buried); three and four layers up to 46 / 77 % at 1 km and 1 kHz (audit W3B-08). per_metre_mutual_impedance gives the exact layered earth-return impedance per metre (within 5e-8 of Carson and of the two-layer Pollaczek integral; against an independent integral within 6e-8 of the earth-return correction up to 300 m and 7e-7 at 1 km for buried pairs, audit W3B-08).
    • Q34: the self term of every sommerfeld segment is the exact one-dimensional reduction of its double integral, and close pairs get a graded rule: no step at a quarter skin depth (4.8e-4 off before, with a step of 3.5e-4); with the λ grid of audit W3B-01 the self correction is within 1.1e-9 for the soils tested (3.4e-9 in high-contrast soils, audit W3R2B-08) and close pairs within about 4e-8 (the stream had stated 2.7e-9; up to 1.5e-3 before).
    • Q39: BusType documents record the effective-resistivity preset (rho_preset= of the exporters, metadata["rho_preset"], BusTypeSpec.rho_preset); evaluate_spec and bustype_from_spec warn with RhoPresetMismatchWarning when the bus uses another preset.
    • Overlaps: diagnose() reports inductive conductors that lie beside each other closer than touching over more than ten touching distances and 5 % of the shorter one (category overlap); the generated networks had such pairs (the 5-house radial network 10, 200 houses 247: drops of one tap on one axis), which led to decision D9.
    • Cost: a long lumped conductor under sommerfeld costs at most linear time with bounded memory (W2B-05: 2 km 28 s and 1.6 GB → 1.1 s and 0.21 GB; 100 km 10.6 s within 0.24 GB, but beyond 33 km for that conductor, 38 km for one at 0.7 m, the result warns with SommerfeldResolutionWarning and is not converged, audit W3B-10). Compiled Carson integrals were measured and not built (5 … 13 % of an inductive sweep after memoisation, below the 20 % threshold; 10.63).
    • carson_series: a tilted near-parallel pair that comes close is tiled per stretch (W1R3-07: a lumped pair and its pieces within 8e-6 of the loop reactance); generated networks move by up to 3e-7 (audit W3B-07). On the generated networks of 5 … 100 houses the series reads X 4 … 21 % and R up to 11 % high at 1 kHz and X 21 … 50 % and R up to 0.9 % high at 50 Hz against sommerfeld (audits W3B-02, W3R2B-03).
    • Golden: five cases move by 8e-10 … 4.9e-7 (cond_lumped_long_pen_sommerfeld 4.9e-7, cond_isolated_pen_sommerfeld_two_layer, cond_overhead_lead_over_pen_carson_series, cond_galvanic_neumann_sommerfeld, defaults_mixed_cluster_inductive).

    Audits W3A and W3B of the wave-3 streams¶

    Two auditors attacked wave 3 (snapshot 0b942c9), W3A stream w3xl, W3B stream w3perf together with the fix streams fw1r3 and fw2r2. Findings:

    • W3A-01 (medium): the image work grew with the world's longest segment and with the image density: eight 9.4 m pieces among 1920 segments made the solve 2.7 … 6.5 times slower, thin top layers 7 … 8.5 times, and the TN 40-house network in 1000/30 Ω·m with h₁ = 1.5 m took 4.4 times (11 times without numba) the solve before wave 3, where the texts said +59 … +70 % and 3.3 times.
    • W3A-02 (medium, pre-existing): image pairs used the reduced kernel √(r² + a²): a ring 1 cm above a 1000/30 Ω·m interface read +1.60 % against the exact thin ring (+1.74 % before wave 3); the cause of what stream w4axi had found.
    • W3A-03 … W3A-06 (low): the strip bound "within 7.7e-7, monotonically" held at the five sampled lengths only; stale texts on the one-step switch and on far images as point sources, 6.2e-4 instead of "up to 4e-4" and 4.7e-11 instead of 3e-11 at UTM coordinates; the memory claims held with numba only (161 instead of 79.6 MiB); the near-interface "finer segment_length" warning fired for the line kernel.
    • W3A-07 (info): switching the quadrupole term off did not give the code before wave 3.
    • W3B-01 (low): the Q34 bound failed for short segments (1e-5 … 7.4e-5 at 0.01 skin depths, 7.5e-5 … 3e-4 at 1 m), because the logarithmic region of the λ grid had 64 nodes; pre-existing, at most 8e-9 in a solved reactance; the close rule 3.5e-8 off instead of the stated 2.7e-9.
    • W3B-02 (medium, text): carson_series on generated networks of 5 … 30 houses reads X +3.6 … +20.7 % and R +1.9 … +7.8 % at 1 kHz and X +21 … +44 % at 50 Hz, which no text stated.
    • W3B-03 (low): the overlap check had no tolerance: cables 1e-14 m inside the touching distance were flagged over their whole length.
    • W3B-04 (info, decision): drops of one tap in a row at the touching distance move the 5-house radial network's X at 1 kHz by −2.0 / −2.7 % (decision D9).
    • W3B-05, W3B-07 … W3B-09 (low, texts): star networks warn too, and the stream's limitation and roadmap texts were not taken over; the stretch tiling moves generated networks (the 10-house star with sites 2.8e-7, the 80-house radial network 3.4e-8); the errors of penetration_depth in three and four layers (44 … 77 % at 1 km, 1 kHz) and per_metre_mutual_impedance 6.8e-7 off at 1 km for buried pairs; rows 34 and 39 of this page.
    • W3B-06 (low): to_bustype and save_bustype_to_db validated the preset and dropped it.
    • W3B-10 … W3B-12 (info): the 100 km conductor warns as unconverged, and so does one of 37 km; the mutual of two long close conductors moved by 2.6e-5 … 4e-5, not 8e-6; penetration_depth read "50" and True as frequencies and raised ZeroDivisionError at 1e-320 Hz, and PenetrationDepth could not be compared or hashed; a proposed test of audit W2R2 reads a phrase that D2 removed.

    Confirmed: the blend is continuous; translation invariance holds (7.7e-14 over local shifts, 4.7e-11 at UTM coordinates); the cross-layer rod is within 1.6e-6 of an exact closed form; chunking is bit-identical; golden 497 quantities identical, 6 within round-off, 60 moved within their bounds; the jump of a ring on an interface that w4axi found is resolved (≤ 0.0005 %). penetration_depth agrees with the auditor's own reflection coefficient to 1.8e-9 in one to four layers; Q34 has no step (≤ 6.5e-12 over 6700 lengths); W2B-05 is bounded (2 km: 1.22 s, 0.21 GB); fw1r3 and fw2r2 are bit-identical in the golden cases and right in the auditor's own attacks (pieces against the lumped pair ≤ 4.6e-15; W2R2-02 within 1.4e-14 of the former path). Taken up by fw3xl (W3A, with the far-image option), fw3perf (W3B-01 … -08, -10, -11; -04 with D9) and the coordinator (W3B-09, -12).

    Streams fw3xl and fw3perf: fixes of audits W3A and W3B, the far-image option and D9¶

    • fw3xl (W3A-01, -05): the far-image work follows the pair class and the upper half of the symmetric matrix (bit-identical, round-off in the symmetric half): 16 house grids take 3.8 times the point kernel in homogeneous soil and 1.6 times in two-layer soil (h₁ = 5 m) with numba (5.8 and 1.4 times without); against the solve before wave 3 the TN 40-house network takes 1.84 times with numba and 3.63 times without (4.2 and 9.7 times before the fix), eight 9.4 m pieces among the 16 house grids 1.5 times (2.8), thin top layers 3.6 … 4.5 times (7 … 8.7). Without numba the far-image term holds 80 instead of 161 MiB.
    • fw3xl (W3A-02, -03, -04, -06): image pairs act at the transverse distance max(ρ⊥, a), collinear images and images within one wire radius of their segment keep the reduced kernel, far images have no radius term, and the image corrections are symmetric: the ring 1 cm above 1000/30 Ω·m −0.004 % instead of +1.60 %, a strip 0.1 m above the interface −3.8e-6 instead of +6.8e-5, the rod through the interface within 1.4e-6 of its exact value. The near-interface warning fires for the point kernel only; the texts are corrected.
    • The far-image option (the author's decision of 2026-11-15): Engine.image_far_field = "point" keeps the far Tagg/Sunde images and the images of the cross-layer correction beyond the near-pair reach as point sources, the continuous switch and the transverse distance stay; networks move by 2e-5 … 1.8e-4 against the default (16 house grids 0.4 m above a 1000/10 Ω·m interface 4.25e-4, audit W3R2A-04), surface potentials by up to 0.8 %, and the TN 40-house network solves in 0.46 of the default's time (0.84 of the solve before wave 3); recorded in metadata["series"]["far_field"]. Since stream fw3r2a the point form keeps the near image pairs on the cross-layer path: networks 3e-6 … 4.3e-4, the TN 40-house network 0.49 of the default's time and 0.91 of the solve before wave 3.
    • Golden (fw3xl): W3A-02 moves four cases by at most 3.2e-7 (line_img2_ring_rods_within_layer; 3.19e-7, audit W3R2A-07); the other fixes and the option are bit-identical in every stored case.
    • fw3perf (W3B): the logarithmic region of the λ grid has 256 nodes: the self term is within 1.1e-9 of a refined rule and an independent reference from 1 m and 0.01 skin depths to 3 km, close pairs within about 4e-8, at no measurable cost. The overlap check allows round-off at the touching distance, and find_close_wires calls such wires close; the live BusType keeps the preset as the last line of its description (groundfield rho_preset: <name>, read back by bustype_rho_preset); penetration_depth refuses bool, str, complex and subnormal frequencies, and PenetrationDepth compares by value and is hashable; texts of W3B-02, -05, -07, -08, -10. Golden: the five sommerfeld cases move by 4.8e-18 … 2.2e-11.
    • fw3perf (D9): service drops that leave one tap in one direction lie side by side at the touching distance, sorted by length and centred on their former axis, so that only neighbours touch (PlannedBranch.axis_offset_m): X at 1 kHz of the radial 5-house network −2.33 / −2.70 % (carson_series / sommerfeld), radial 10 −2.31 / −2.82 %, star 10 −1.06 / −1.27 %, radial 80 / 200 ≤ 5e-4, radial 30 ≤ 1.1e-6; no golden case moves.

    Audits W3R2A and W3R2B: round 2 on the wave-3 fixes¶

    W3R2A attacked fw3xl and the far-image option (snapshot ac556ec), W3R2B fw3perf and D9 (snapshot df16521). Findings:

    • W3R2A-01 (medium): with image_far_field="point" wires wholly below the layer interface lose accuracy as their segments get longer: a 75 m strip below a 0.5 m top layer reads −1.0e-3 … −1.8e-3 at 2.5 m segments, −1.7 … −4.6 % at 7.5 m and up to −58 % at 37.5 m against an exact reference (the default within 1.3e-6); 16 house grids with a 75 m bare conductor in 40 m pieces +1.13 % against the default.
    • W3R2A-02 (low): far_field of the public solve_image_2layer is not validated; any other value runs the point form, and the field then disagrees with the solve.
    • W3R2A-03 (low): within one wire radius of the interface Z is not monotone in the gap (a ring in 1000/30 Ω·m: 5.993 → 7.319 → 5.992 Ω).
    • W3R2A-04 (low, texts): "the point form moves networks by 2e-5 … 1.8e-4" is too narrow (4.25e-4), and "as before wave 3" is misleading (up to 2.3e-4 from the D2 blend, which the option keeps).
    • W3R2A-05 … W3R2A-07 (info): "integrated exactly" overstates the axis value for short strips near the interface (a 2 m strip 5 mm above 1000/30 Ω·m +3.0e-4); direct pairs of different wires keep the reduced kernel (two strips 1 cm above the interface, 5 cm apart, −3.5e-4); a bound comment in w3xl.toml (3.4e-7 against the measured 3.19e-7).
    • W3R2B-01 (medium): the D9 rows held only to 1e-11 m: any placement jitter from 1 nm to 1 mm put the drops back on one axis (the 10-house radial network: 19 / 20 / 14 overlaps), and X at 1 kHz jumped by +2.37 % / +2.90 % (carson_series / sommerfeld) at 1 nm.
    • W3R2B-02 (low): the overlap tolerance of 1e-11 m lies below the spacing of floating-point numbers at projected coordinates: rows translated to (5e5, 5.8e6) give 4 / 10 / 98 false overlaps for the radial networks of 5 / 80 / 200 houses.
    • W3R2B-03 (medium, text): the stated carson_series error is exceeded (the 80-house radial network +49.7 % at 50 Hz; R up to +11.1 % at 1 kHz and 0.9 % at 50 Hz; the default sommerfeld 1.0e-3 from a refined one, stated 4e-4).
    • W3R2B-04 (low, text): the base error of a 1 m self term is 3.0e-4 / 6.4e-4 in 30/1000 and 10/10 000 Ω·m, not 7.5e-5.
    • W3R2B-05 (info): a PenetrationDepth built directly is not validated, and f = +0.0 and −0.0 compare equal but hash differently.
    • W3R2B-06 (low): loading refused only blank presets ("station\nfoo" lost the preset, " station " read back as "station", unknown names passed), and every user line that merely starts with the marker was deleted.
    • W3R2B-07, W3R2B-08 (info): the start points of a row leave their tap by up to (n − 1)/2 touching distances, and stars of 40 … 180 houses have rows too (the 60-house star X −1.0e-3); the self-term bound of 1.1e-9 reaches 3.4e-9 in 10/10 000 Ω·m and 1.8e-9 under a 0.3 m top layer.

    Confirmed: the paths of the option (round trips, several frequencies, solve_mutual_field, no effect with the point kernel, the mom family or homogeneous soil); with D2 and W3A-02 switched off at run time the point form reproduces the code before wave 3 to 6.6e-7 in the upper layer and 5.7e-6 on the cross-layer path; the W3A-01 matrices are bit-identical but for the diagonal rule (Z −5.4e-14); W3A-02 against the auditor's exact references (strips with a gap of at least one radius ≤ 5.5e-5, the ring −0.0039 %, reciprocity ≤ 4e-11). The D9 geometry of 80 AP1 networks and 58 variants: every conductor that is not a row drop, every electrode and every junction bit-identical, the rows equal to the auditor's own construction, up to 277 overlaps → 0 without jitter, old plans rebuilt bit-identically; the D9 moves agree between three constructions; W3B-01 holds (self terms within 1.1e-9 for the stream's soils, close pairs 3.5e-8), golden 43 cases bit-identical and 5 within 4.8e-18 … 2.2e-11. Outside the round, W3R2B found that plan() followed by build() drew the placement jitter again and that a tie of the star topology flips under jitter (the 10-house star: X −11.8 %, now on the roadmap). Taken up by fw3r2a (W3R2A), fw3r2b (W3R2B-01, -02, -05, -06, the texts of -03 and -07, plan() / build()) and the coordinator (the texts of W3R2B-04, -08).

    Stream fw3r2a: fixes of audit W3R2A¶

    • The point form below the interface (W3R2A-01): on the cross-layer path image_far_field="point" keeps every image family with all its images; the near pairs within 7.5 segment lengths are Galerkin integrals blended into the point form, a segment's own image follows the switch of every pair, and only the dense far term stays out. The 75 m strip below a 0.5 m top layer is within 1.1e-5 of its exact value at every segment length (−1.06e-5 at 0.5 m in 1000/30 Ω·m, the option's own far-pair error; −3.1e-7 at 37.5 m, where it read −58 %), and 16 house grids with the 75 m conductor in 37.5 m pieces read −3.65e-6 against the default instead of +1.13 %. The default and the series path in point form are bit-identical.
    • Its cost: on cross-layer worlds the point form takes 0.49 … 0.94 of the default's time; below thin top layers it keeps the Galerkin near image pairs it used to save (1.5 … 2.4 times the solve before wave 3 instead of 1.2). The TN 40-house network in 1000/30 Ω·m with h₁ = 1.5 m is a cross-layer world (its transformer rods reach below h₁): its point form moves by 2.3e-4 and takes 0.91 of the solve before wave 3 (0.83 before the fix).
    • far_field (W3R2A-02): solve_image_2layer, the series kernel, the image families and the field correction raise ValueError for any value but "quadrupole" or "point".
    • Within one wire radius (W3R2A-03): the image blend runs over a tenth of the wire radius; the bump of 22 % of the ring in 1000/30 Ω·m is gone, and a step of at most 0.17 % onto the interface remains (wires 0 < g < a/2 from the interface move by up to −18 %: the ring 1.75 mm above, 7.319 → 5.985 Ω; the ring 1 cm above stays at −0.004 %).
    • Texts (W3R2A-04 … -07): the point form moves networks by 3e-6 … 4.3e-4 against the default (16 house grids 0.4 m above a 1000/10 Ω·m interface) and is not the result before wave 3 (16 grids in 1000/30 Ω·m, h₁ = 1.5 m: +2.32e-4); the line kernel is exact for long wires with an end effect O(a²/(d·ℓ)) (a 2 m strip at one wire radius +3.03e-4); direct pairs of different wires keep their convention (−3.50e-4 against a circumference-averaged direct term); the golden comment gives 3.19e-7.
